Severity:  MED
Feed:      https://feeds.bloomberg.com/technology/news.rss

OpenAI Weighs IPO in 2027 | Bloomberg Tech 6/26/2026

Bloomberg’s Ed Ludlow breaks down OpenAI's decision to potentially hold off on an IPO until next year - sending shares of SoftBank falling. Plus, global tech stocks drop after Apple hikes hardware price due to a memory crunch, and SpaceX's $25 billion bond sale is off to a rocky start. (Source: Bloomberg)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
Bloomberg Tech (6/26/2026) reports OpenAI may delay its IPO until 2027, which sent SoftBank shares falling. The same segment notes global tech stocks dropping after Apple hiked hardware prices on a memory crunch, and a rocky start to SpaceX's $25B bond sale.

内容:
Bloomberg Tech（2026/6/26）报道 OpenAI 可能将 IPO 推迟到 2027 年，消息导致 SoftBank 股价下跌。同一节目还提到全球科技股因 Apple 在内存紧缺下上调硬件价格而走低，以及 SpaceX 250 亿美元债券发行开局不顺。

Why it matters:
An IPO that keeps slipping signals OpenAI isn't ready to face public-market scrutiny of its cash burn, and SoftBank's drop shows how levered the AI-infra complex is to that single outcome. The memory-crunch price hike and the strained SpaceX bond sale point to the same stress — capital and components for the buildout are getting scarcer and pricier.

影响:
IPO 一再推迟，说明 OpenAI 还没准备好让公开市场审视其现金消耗，而 SoftBank 应声下跌则暴露出整个 AI 基础设施板块对这一单一结果的高度杠杆敞口。内存紧缺导致的涨价和 SpaceX 债券发行受阻指向同一种压力——这轮建设所需的资金和零部件正变得更稀缺、更昂贵。

AI Cost Reality Check Hits Asia Tech Stocks as Apple Hikes Prices

Asian technology stocks slumped after Apple Inc. and Microsoft Corp. raised prices for their products, stoking concern that rising component costs will curb demand for devices and eventually slow the memory chip rally that has powered much of the AI trade.
Bloomberg Tech Host Ed Ludlow discusses the move from Apple to raise prices as well as what's next for OpenAI as they mull a delay for the company's IPO. (Source: Bloomberg)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
Bloomberg reports Asian tech stocks slumped after Apple and Microsoft raised product prices, with coverage also flagging that OpenAI is weighing a delay to its IPO.

内容:
据 Bloomberg 报道，Apple 与 Microsoft 上调产品价格后亚洲科技股下挫，报道同时提及 OpenAI 正在考虑推迟 IPO。

Why it matters:
Price hikes signal rising component costs feeding through to devices, threatening the memory-chip rally that underpins much of the AI trade. A mooted OpenAI IPO delay is a demand-side tell — the marquee AI name hesitating to test public markets undercuts the funding-and-momentum story the bull case relies on.

影响:
涨价意味着零部件成本上升正传导至终端设备，威胁到支撑大部分 AI 交易的存储芯片涨势。OpenAI 传出推迟 IPO 则是需求端的信号——这家头牌 AI 公司迟迟不敢上市检验公开市场，削弱了多头所依赖的融资与动能叙事。

Morgan Stanley, Goldman Shares Fall on Possible OpenAI IPO Delay

Shares of Morgan Stanley and Goldman Sachs Group Inc. fell on Friday after OpenAI is said to weigh holding off on an initial public offering until next year amid volatility in technology stocks.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
Morgan Stanley and Goldman Sachs shares fell Friday after reports OpenAI may delay its IPO until next year amid tech-stock volatility.

内容:
在有消息称 OpenAI 可能因科技股波动将 IPO 推迟到明年后，Morgan Stanley 和 Goldman Sachs 股价周五下跌。

Why it matters:
The two banks are lead underwriters on the most anticipated AI-era listing; pricing their stocks down on a delay rumor shows the market treats the OpenAI IPO as a load-bearing liquidity event. A slip signals the public exit for AI capital is being pushed out, tightening the window for late-stage investors to mark gains.

影响:
这两家投行是这场 AI 时代最受瞩目上市案的主承销商；它们的股价因一则推迟传闻而走低，说明市场把 OpenAI 的 IPO 当成了一个举足轻重的流动性事件。推迟意味着 AI 资本的公开退出通道被往后挪，后期投资者兑现收益的窗口随之收窄。

What this means:
A CNBC report (via TradingView) says OpenAI is not on an IPO timeline yet and has not begun 'testing-waters' meetings with investors.

内容:

OpenAI is considering an initial public offering as soon as in 2027, according to people familiar with the matter, a timeline that would potentially see it go public after its artificial intelligence rival Anthropic PBC.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
OpenAI is weighing an IPO as soon as 2027, a timeline that would put it public after rival Anthropic PBC's expected debut.

内容:

AI 数据中心对 HBM/DRAM 的需求正在挤占消费电子的供应，把短缺传导到终端产品价格上。当 AI 资本开支开始明显抬高无关硬件的成本时，说明这轮建设已经在挤压整个内存供应链——这是泡沫向实体经济外溢的信号。

SoftBank Group’s stock tumbled the most since August 2024 after reports that OpenAI may hold off on an initial public offering until next year, delaying returns for its Japanese backer. Neil Campling reports on Bloomberg Television. (Source: Bloomberg)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
SoftBank Group shares fell the most since August 2024 after reports that OpenAI may delay its IPO until next year, pushing back returns for its largest Japanese backer.

内容:

Why it matters:

影响:

影响:

Tim Cook calls recent memory chip price surge unprecedented, compares it to a 'hundred-year flood' - Crypto Briefing

<a href="https://news.google.com/rss/articles/CBMif0FVX3lxTE4yaFpUc2FnZDNpQkRjWmtqQnBNMmVGQUpsWTFGWENSbWlxVVNaeXQ1NTllQnVSVHBoQ1J4VFR5TXp1bmgyazlyQzdFaDFHMU05VjA0TGNSNTR2UUprNnNzSlN5bmsxLUhZYnlSeEFSZmItU1FSUW9zVU5namdPZGs?oc=5" target="_blank">Tim Cook calls recent memory chip price surge unprecedented, compares it to a 'hundred-year flood'</a>&nbsp;&nbsp;<font color="#6f6f6f">Crypto Briefing</font>

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
Apple CEO Tim Cook called the recent memory chip price surge "unprecedented," comparing it to a "hundred-year flood."

内容:
苹果 CEO Tim Cook 称近期内存芯片涨价"前所未见"，并将其比作"百年一遇的洪水"。

Why it matters:
Memory (DRAM/HBM) is being soaked up by AI datacenter buildout, and pricing is now spilling into consumer hardware costs. When even Apple — a top-tier buyer with huge leverage — flags supply stress this loudly, it signals the AI-capex boom is straining the component supply chain and squeezing margins downstream.

影响:
内存（DRAM/HBM）正被 AI 数据中心建设大量吸走，涨价压力已经传导到消费级硬件成本。连苹果这种采购量巨大、议价能力顶尖的买家都如此高调地示警供应紧张，说明 AI capex 热潮正在挤压整条零部件供应链，并向下游侵蚀利润。

内容:

Why it matters:

影响:
内存（DRAM/HBM）正被 AI 数据中心建设大量吸走，短缺已经传导到消费电子的定价上。当 AI capex 开始明显推高全球最大消费电子采购商的成本时，说明供给已经紧张、这笔支出正在挤占其他需求——这是基建热潮渗入实体经济的真实信号。

MED C10 [C10-MED] 10y Real Yield rising: 2.23% (+51bp off 90d-low) 2026-06-25 22:29 UTC 
Series:   REAL10 (DFII10)
Date:     2026-06-24
Current:  2.23%
90d low:  1.72%
Move:     +51bp off 90d-low (fire ≥ +40bp)

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The 10-year real yield (REAL10) spiked to 2.23% on 2026-06-24, up 0.51 points from its trailing low of 1.72%.

内容:
10 年期实际收益率 (REAL10) 在 2026-06-24 跳升至 2.23%，较此前低点 1.72% 上升了 0.51 个百分点。

Why it matters:
Higher real yields raise the discount rate on long-duration AI-capex bets, where payback is years out. A 51bp jump compresses the present value of future data-center returns and tightens financing for debt-funded buildouts — direct pressure on the bubble.

影响:
实际收益率走高抬升了对长久期 AI capex 押注的贴现率，而这类投资的回本周期本就要好几年。51 个基点的跳升压缩了未来数据中心收益的现值，也收紧了靠举债扩张的融资条件——对泡沫构成直接压力。

OpenAI 的 IPO 是整个 AI capex 周期所倚赖的标志性流动性事件——用来验证一级市场估值并为下一轮支出筹资。推迟意味着公开市场尚未准备好消化这个泡沫中最大的名字，这会让融资链条继续困在私募端，更显脆弱。

OpenAI Leans Toward Waiting Until 2027 for IPO, NY Times Says

OpenAI is leaning toward holding off on an initial public offering until 2027, the New York Times reported, citing three people involved in the company’s deliberations.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
What this means:
The New York Times reports OpenAI is leaning toward delaying its IPO until 2027, citing three people involved in the company's deliberations.

内容:
据 New York Times 报道，OpenAI 倾向于将 IPO 推迟到 2027 年，消息来自三位参与公司内部讨论的人士。

Why it matters:
Staying private longer keeps OpenAI's true financials — burn rate, margins, compute costs — out of public disclosure, denying the market a hard data point on AI economics. Pushing the IPO out also signals the company doesn't yet want to face public-market scrutiny of its losses, consistent with a spend cycle still far ahead of revenue.

影响:
继续保持私有状态意味着 OpenAI 的真实财务数据——烧钱速度、利润率、算力成本——不必公开披露，市场也就拿不到关于 AI 经济性的硬数据。推迟 IPO 也表明公司还不愿让公开市场审视其亏损，这与支出节奏仍远超收入的状况一致。

内容:
